Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in tissue theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 656 and the invariant I(A,B) = 178 mod 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_656(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 656. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^178(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)
